Nov 27: Newell's Old Boys 3 points deducted at the end of championship.
Aug 7, 2018: Newell's Old Boys, two points were returned.
Sep 14, 2018: Arsenal 2 points deducted.
Sep 14, 2018: Olimpo 1 point deducted.
Nov 26, 2018: Decision of August 7 was revoked. Therefore Newell's Old Boys had three points deducted.
Jun 28, 2019: TAS fixed the sanction as Aug 7, 2018: Newell's Old Boys only one point deducted.
